Design decision to make: the replay fixture key must be deterministic. Key = shortHash(stableStringify({model, system, messages, schemaName, temperature}))? But if model changes, fixtures invalidate. Better key on the semantic content: promptDigest.

Key insight for the competition: recording mode writes fixtures when a real API key exists; replay mode reads them. And there's a third mode: `record` requires a key.

2. Replay client: on `complete(req)`, compute digest, look for `fixtures/llm/<digest>.json`. If found → return. If not found and mode=replay → throw a clear error naming the missing digest (fail loudly, never silently fabricate). If mode=record/live → call real API, persist, return.

- `mode`: `live` | `record` | `replay` from CROSSEXAM_MODE env (default replay)
  - `live`: call API, don't persist
  - `record`: call API, persist
  - `replay`: read fixtures only

types.ts:

```ts
/** LLM 适配层的公共契约。 */

export type LlmMode = 'live' | 'record' | 'replay';

export interface LlmMessage {
  role: 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
}

export interface LlmRequest {
  /** 人类可读的用途标签，只为排查方便，不参与 fixture 指纹计算。 */
  purpose: string;
  system: string;
  messages: readonly LlmMessage[];
  temperature?: number;
  maxTokens?: number;
  /** 要求模型只输出 JSON；具体结构由 shared/schemas.ts 校验。 */
  json?: boolean;
}

export interface LlmUsage {
  inputTokens: number;
  outputTokens: number;
  cacheReadTokens: number;
  reasoningTokens: number;
}

export const EMPTY_USAGE: LlmUsage = { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, cacheReadTokens: 0, reasoningTokens: 0 };

export interface LlmResult {
  text: string;
  usage: LlmUsage;
  /** fixture 指纹：同样的提示词必然得到同样的指纹，回放靠它对齐。 */
  digest: string;
  /** true 表示这次结果来自本地 fixture，没有发出网络请求。 */
  replayed: boolean;
  /** 真的调用模型时经过的毫秒数；回放为 0。 */
  elapsedMs: number;
}

export type LlmStreamEvent =
  | { type: 'delta'; text: string }
  | { type: 'done'; result: LlmResult };

export interface LlmClient {
  readonly mode: LlmMode;
  complete(req: LlmRequest): Promise<LlmResult>;
  stream(req: LlmRequest): AsyncGenerator<LlmStreamEvent, void, void>;
}

export class LlmError extends Error {
  readonly code: string;
  constructor(code: string, message: string) { super(message); this.name = 'LlmError'; this.code = code; }
}
```

Wait: `erasableSyntaxOnly` forbids parameter properties (`constructor(private x)`) — I'm not using them. But it also... class fields with declarations are fine.

cassette.ts:

```ts
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { shortHash, stableStringify } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { LlmRequest, LlmUsage } from './types.ts';

export const CASSETTE_VERSION = 1;

export interface CassetteEntry {
  version: number;
  digest: string;
  purpose: string;
  model: string;
  /** 记录时间，给人看的，不参与指纹。 */
  recordedAt: string;
  request: { system: string; messages: {role,content}[]; temperature?: number; maxTokens?: number; json?: boolean };
  response: { text: string; usage: LlmUsage; chunks?: string[] };
}

export function digestFor(req: LlmRequest): string {
  return shortHash(stableStringify({
    system: req.system,
    messages: req.messages,
    temperature: req.temperature ?? null,
    maxTokens: req.maxTokens ?? null,
    json: req.json ?? false,
  }), 24);
}
```

Node 24 can import a .ts file directly? Node's type stripping works for .ts files it loads. The "exports" maps to "./src/index.ts" — Node will load a .ts file and strip types. With `--experimental-strip-types` default in Node 22.18+/24? In Node 24, type stripping is enabled by default (unflagged since 23.6). Yes: Node 23.6+ has type stripping on by default. Node 24 → on by default.

But vitest (vite) resolves bare specifiers via node_modules symlink; with `exports` → ./src/index.ts it should work.

replay.ts:

```ts
/**
 * 回放客户端：只读卡带，绝不联网。
 * ...
 */
import { LlmError } from './types.ts';
import type { LlmClient, LlmMode, LlmRequest, LlmResult, LlmStreamEvent } from './types.ts';
import { digestFor, readCassette } from './cassette.ts';
import type { CassetteEntry } from './cassette.ts';

export const REPLAY_CHUNK_SIZE = 24;

/** 把整段文本切成确定性的分片，让回放也能有流式的观感。 */
export function chunkText(text: string, size = REPLAY_CHUNK_SIZE): string[] { ... }

export function createReplayClient(dir: string): LlmClient { ... }
```

chunkText: iterate over Array.from(text) (codepoints), accumulate up to size, but prefer to cut after a newline or sentence punctuation if within the window. Simple deterministic rule:

```ts
const BREAKS = new Set(['\n', '。', '！', '？', '；', '.', '!', '?', ';', '，', ',']);
export function chunkText(text: string, size = REPLAY_CHUNK_SIZE): string[] {
  const chars = Array.from(text);
  const out: string[] = [];
  let buf: string[] = [];
  for (const ch of chars) {
    buf.push(ch);
    const atBreak = BREAKS.has(ch) && buf.length >= Math.floor(size / 2);
    if (buf.length >= size || atBreak) { out.push(buf.join('')); buf = []; }
  }
  if (buf.length > 0) out.push(buf.join(''));
  return out;
}
```
Deterministic. Good.

createReplayClient:
```ts
export function createReplayClient(dir: string, mode: LlmMode = 'replay'): LlmClient {
  const load = (req: LlmRequest): { entry: CassetteEntry; digest: string } => {
    const digest = digestFor(req);
    const entry = readCassette(dir, digest);
    if (!entry) throw new LlmError('cassette-missing', '...');
    return { entry, digest };
  };

  return {
    mode,
    async complete(req) {
      const { entry, digest } = load(req);
      return { text: entry.response.text, usage: entry.response.usage, digest, replayed: true, elapsedMs: 0 };
    },
    async *stream(req) {
      const { entry, digest } = load(req);
      const chunks = entry.response.chunks && entry.response.chunks.length > 0 ? entry.response.chunks : chunkText(entry.response.text);
      for (const c of chunks) yield { type: 'delta', text: c };
      yield { type: 'done', result: { text: entry.response.text, usage: entry.response.usage, digest, replayed: true, elapsedMs: 0 } };
    },
  };
}
```

Hmm — `mode` is passed as 'replay' always; but the naming: createReplayClient(dir, mode) lets us label. Just use 'replay'.

Wait: `mode` for the replay client used in `live` mode? No.

Missing-cassette error message should be actionable, include purpose + digest.

```ts
/** 测试与调用方共用：把一次可能失败的调用收敛成 LlmError（不吞掉非 LlmError）。 */
export async function classifyLlmError(fn: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<LlmError> {
  try {
    await fn();
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof LlmError) return err;
    throw err;
  }
  throw new Error('预期这次调用会失败，但它成功了');
}
```

```markdown
# fixtures/ — 离线回放记录

这里存放 LLM 调用的真实记录（"卡带"），文件名是**提示词内容的指纹**。

## 为什么要有这个目录

比赛要求「10 分钟极速复现」。评委机器上不会有 API Key，也不该为了看一个
demo 去申请一个。所以 CrossExam 默认运行在 `replay` 模式：
所有模型调用改从本目录读取当时的真实响应，一次网络请求都不发。

指纹只由提示词决定，**不含模型名**——换模型不会让整盘卡带失效。

## 目录规则

- 每一条记录的文件名 = `digestFor(request)` = 提示词内容的 sha256 前 24 位
- 记录里含当时的 `usage`、`purpose`、`model` 与流式分片，供追溯
- 缺失记录时回放会**报错**而不是静默降级（见 `src/llm/replay.ts`）

## 隐私红线（必须遵守）

**只允许用仓库里的示例简历录制卡带。**
真实个人简历产生的记录一律不得提交——简历是个人隐私数据，
本项目的约定是「只落本地 SQLite，不上传任何第三方存储」，
把内容写进公开仓库等于把这条承诺作废。

录制前先确认 `.env` 里的 `CROSSEXAM_MODE=record`，并用示例数据跑。
```

msg1:
```
chore(server): 建立 server 包骨架并接入 npm workspaces

npm workspaces 会把 packages/server 链接进根 node_modules，
于是服务端可以直接 import '@crossexam/shared'，不需要任何路径别名或打包步骤。

zod 是服务端自己的直接依赖：shared 的 schema 是给编排层用的，
服务端也会在边界上再校验一次，不应该借 shared 的依赖。

hono 与 @hono/node-server 先不加——P2 真的写路由时再加，
免得骨架里躺着一堆还没被引用到的依赖。
```

Wait — I didn't add hono. Right, deps are only @crossexam/shared and zod. Good, matches message.

msg2:
```
feat(server): 定义 LLM 适配层契约，把模型调用变成可替换的一层

这是「10 分钟复现」能不能成立的关键一步。如果业务代码直接 fetch 模型接口，
评委没有 API Key 就一步都跑不动。

所以模型调用先收敛成一个接口：complete 与 stream，配上 live / record / replay
三种模式。业务层只认接口，不认谁在下面接。
LlmError 带错误码而不是只有文案，是为了让上层能区分
「卡带缺记录」和「网络真的挂了」——这两种失败的处置方式完全不同。
```

msg3:
```
feat(server): 卡带读写，用提示词指纹寻址

记录文件名 = 提示词内容的 sha256 前 24 位。用 stableStringify 保证对象键顺序
不影响结果，否则同一份提示词换个字段顺序就会变成另一盘卡带。

指纹刻意不含模型名与用途标签：
换模型不该让整盘卡带失效，而「这次是谁问的」属于运行时元数据，
记在文件里给人看即可，不该参与寻址。

版本号与「内容与文件名必须一致」两道检查都写成硬失败。
卡带是复现的唯一依据，宁可炸掉也不能读到一份半对的东西。
```

msg4:
```
feat(server): 只读回放客户端，无 API Key 也能跑通全流程

回放客户端有一条硬规则：**找不到记录就报错**，
绝不返回空结果、也绝不静默降级去调真接口。
静默降级会让「离线可跑」变成一句谎言——流程看着跑通了，
实际每一环都在拿空气当输入，而错误会被推迟到很远的地方才爆出来。

流式回放优先使用录制时记下的分片，没有分片才用 chunkText 现切。
chunkText 是纯函数，因此回放不止结果可复现，连前端的流式观感也可复现。

fixtures/README.md 同时写下隐私红线：只允许用示例简历录制卡带。
```

Let me commit.

```ts
/**
 * 真实调用客户端（OpenAI 兼容 /chat/completions），并可选把每次调用录成卡带。
 *
 * 为什么自己写 SSE 解析而不上 SDK：这一层要能被完整看懂，
 * 而且复现性要求"录下来的分片必须和当时推给前端的一模一样"——
 * 只有自己攒分片才能做到这点。
 */

import { writeCassette, digestFor, defaultCassetteDir } from './cassette.ts';
import { EMPTY_USAGE, LlmError } from './types.ts';
import type { LlmClient, LlmRequest, LlmResult, LlmStreamEvent, LlmUsage } from './types.ts';
import type { CassetteEntry } from './cassette.ts';

export interface LiveOptions {
  baseUrl?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
  model?: string;
  /** 给了就录卡带；录制目录默认 fixtures/llm。 */
  recordDir?: string;
  timeoutMs?: number;
  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
  now?: () => Date;
}

export interface LiveConfig {
  baseUrl: string;
  apiKey: string;
  model: string;
  timeoutMs: number;
}

const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120000;

export function resolveLiveConfig(opts: LiveOptions): LiveConfig {
  const baseUrl = (opts.baseUrl ?? process.env['LLM_BASE_URL'] ?? '').trim();
  const apiKey = (opts.apiKey ?? process.env['LLM_API_KEY'] ?? '').trim();
  const model = (opts.model ?? process.env['LLM_MODEL'] ?? '').trim();
  const missing: string[] = [];
  if (baseUrl === '') missing.push('LLM_BASE_URL');
  if (apiKey === '') missing.push('LLM_API_KEY');
  if (model === '') missing.push('LLM_MODEL');
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    throw new LlmError('not-configured', '真实调用缺少配置：' + missing.join('、') + '。若只是想看流程，请用 CROSSEXAM_MODE=replay。');
  }
  return { baseUrl: baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, ''), apiKey: apiKey, model: model, timeoutMs: opts.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS };
}
```

Careful: `/\/+$/` regex contains no backtick. Fine.

Then:
```ts
export function endpointOf(baseUrl: string): string {
  if (baseUrl.endsWith('/chat/completions')) return baseUrl;
  return baseUrl + '/chat/completions';
}
```

usage extraction:
```ts
function num(v: unknown): number { return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : 0; }

export function readUsage(raw: unknown): LlmUsage {
  if (raw === null || typeof raw !== 'object') return { ...EMPTY_USAGE };
  const u = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  const details = (u['prompt_tokens_details'] ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
  const comp = (u['completion_tokens_details'] ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
  const cached = u['prompt_cache_hit_tokens'] ?? (details ? details['cached_tokens'] : 0);
  const reasoning = u['reasoning_tokens'] ?? (comp ? comp['reasoning_tokens'] : 0);
  return {
    inputTokens: num(u['prompt_tokens']),
    outputTokens: num(u['completion_tokens']),
    cacheReadTokens: num(cached),
    reasoningTokens: num(reasoning),
  };
}
```

payload:
```ts
function buildPayload(req: LlmRequest, model: string, stream: boolean): Record<string, unknown> {
  const messages = [{ role: 'system', content: req.system }];
  for (const m of req.messages) messages.push({ role: m.role, content: m.content });
  const payload: Record<string, unknown> = { model: model, messages: messages, stream: stream };
  if (typeof req.temperature === 'number') payload['temperature'] = req.temperature;
  if (typeof req.maxTokens === 'number') payload['max_tokens'] = req.maxTokens;
  if (req.json) payload['response_format'] = { type: 'json_object' };
  if (stream) payload['stream_options'] = { include_usage: true };
  return payload;
}
```

Then request sending with timeout via AbortController.

```ts
async function postJson(cfg, payload, fetchImpl): Promise<Response> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(function () { controller.abort(); }, cfg.timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetchImpl(endpointOf(cfg.baseUrl), {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', authorization: 'Bearer ' + cfg.apiKey },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    return res;
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
```

Hmm — for streaming, the timer must stay alive during the stream read, not cleared after headers. So separate handling: for stream I keep the controller and clear in the outer finally.

Let me structure:

```ts
async function raiseForStatus(res: Response): Promise<never> {
  const body = await res.text().catch(() => '');
  const snippet = body.slice(0, 400);
  const code = res.status === 400 || res.status === 422 ? 'bad-request' : 'http-error';
  throw new LlmError(code, '模型接口返回 ' + res.status + '：' + snippet);
}
```

Note: `await res.text().catch(...)` — Response.text() exists.

Now SSE line parsing. Node's fetch gives a ReadableStream<Uint8Array>. Use `res.body` (may be null). 

```ts
async function *iterSseLines(body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>): AsyncGenerator<string, void, void> {
  const reader = body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buf = '';
  try {
    for (;;) {
      const step = await reader.read();
      if (step.done) break;
      buf += decoder.decode(step.value, { stream: true });
      let idx = buf.indexOf('\n');
      while (idx >= 0) {
        const line = buf.slice(0, idx).replace(/\r$/, '');
        buf = buf.slice(idx + 1);
        yield line;
        idx = buf.indexOf('\n');
      }
    }
    buf += decoder.decode();
    if (buf.length > 0) yield buf.replace(/\r$/, '');
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
}
```

Type annotation `AsyncGenerator<string, void, void>` with `async function *` — erasable. Fine.

index.ts:
```ts
/**
 * LLM 客户端的装配点：按 CROSSEXAM_MODE 决定用哪一个。
 * ...
 */
import { defaultCassetteDir } from './cassette.ts';
import { createLiveClient } from './live.ts';
import { createReplayClient } from './replay.ts';
import { LlmError } from './types.ts';
import type { LlmClient, LlmMode } from './types.ts';
import type { LiveOptions } from './live.ts';

export const DEFAULT_MODE: LlmMode = 'replay';

export function modeFromEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): LlmMode {
  const raw = (env['CROSSEXAM_MODE'] ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (raw === '') return DEFAULT_MODE;
  if (raw === 'live' || raw === 'record' || raw === 'replay') return raw;
  throw new LlmError('not-configured', 'CROSSEXAM_MODE 只能是 live / record / replay，收到「' + raw + '」。');
}
```

Hmm — LlmErrorCode union doesn't include a generic config error; 'not-configured' is fine.

```ts
export interface ClientOptions extends LiveOptions {
  mode?: LlmMode;
  cassetteDir?: string;
}

export function createLlmClient(opts: ClientOptions = {}): LlmClient {
  const mode = opts.mode ?? modeFromEnv();
  const dir = opts.cassetteDir ?? defaultCassetteDir();
  if (mode === 'replay') return createReplayClient(dir);
  if (mode === 'record') return createLiveClient({ ...opts, recordDir: opts.recordDir ?? dir });
  return createLiveClient(opts);
}
```

Also a nice touch: `describeClient(client)` returning a human string for the UI banner (e.g. "离线回放 · 记录来自 fixtures/llm"). Maybe overkill; keep a small `clientLabel`.

```ts
/**
 * LLM 层的装配点：业务代码只 import 这里，不直接碰三个具体客户端。
 *
 * 默认模式是 replay 而不是 live，这是刻意的：
 * 复现指南要求"克隆下来就能跑"，任何需要联网才能启动的默认值
 * 都会把评委挡在门外。要真调模型必须显式设置 CROSSEXAM_MODE。
 */

import { defaultCassetteDir } from './cassette.ts';
import { createLiveClient } from './live.ts';
import { createReplayClient } from './replay.ts';
import { LlmError } from './types.ts';
import type { LiveOptions } from './live.ts';
import type { LlmClient, LlmMode } from './types.ts';

export const DEFAULT_MODE: LlmMode = 'replay';

export const MODE_LABEL: Record<LlmMode, string> = {
  replay: '离线回放（不联网）',
  record: '联网录制（写入卡带）',
  live: '真实调用（不留痕）',
};

export function modeFromEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): LlmMode {...}
```

Hmm `NodeJS.ProcessEnv` requires node types — available. But `Record<string, string|undefined>` is simpler and avoids the global namespace. Use `env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env`.

```ts
export function modeFromEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): LlmMode {
  const raw = (env['CROSSEXAM_MODE'] ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (raw === '') return DEFAULT_MODE;
  if (raw === 'live' || raw === 'record' || raw === 'replay') return raw;
  throw new LlmError('not-configured', 'CROSSEXAM_MODE 只能是 live / record / replay，收到「' + raw + '」。');
}

export interface ClientOptions extends LiveOptions {
  mode?: LlmMode;
  /** 卡带目录；replay 从这里读，record 写到这里。 */
  cassetteDir?: string;
}

export function createLlmClient(opts: ClientOptions = {}): LlmClient {
  const mode = opts.mode ?? modeFromEnv();
  const dir = opts.cassetteDir ?? defaultCassetteDir();
  if (mode === 'replay') return createReplayClient(dir);
  if (mode === 'record') return createLiveClient({ ...opts, recordDir: opts.recordDir ?? dir });
  return createLiveClient(opts);
}

export function describeClient(client: LlmClient, dir: string): string {
  return MODE_LABEL[client.mode] + ' · 卡带目录 ' + dir;
}
```

Note: `MODE_LABEL[client.mode]` with noUncheckedIndexedAccess → string|undefined but Record<LlmMode,string> with a finite key union: noUncheckedIndexedAccess applies to index signatures, not to mapped/literal-key Records. Record<LlmMode,string> where LlmMode is a union of literals produces `{ live: string; record: string; replay: string }` — accessing with a LlmMode-typed key is fine.

`export *` doesn't re-export types under verbatimModuleSyntax? Actually `export *` re-exports both types and values at runtime type-stripping removes type-only... With isolatedModules, `export *` is fine.

Hmm, careful: `export *` from a module that itself does `export type {...}` — fine.

a) feat(server): 把模型的思考过程也纳入流式事件与卡带
```
赛题在用户体验维度点名要「流式、思考可视化」。如果只在 live 时才有思考过程，
离线复现的评委就看不到这一层——而那恰恰是演示视频里最直观的部分。

所以给流式事件加了 reasoning 一种类型，并把思考过程一并记进卡带。
回放时按同样的切分规则推出去，离线与联网的观感一致。

与正文分成两种事件而不是拼在一起，是因为前端要把它们渲染成
两个不同的区域：思考过程应当可折叠、可忽略，正文不行。
```

b) feat(server): 打通真实调用与流式解析，并支持录制卡带
```
自己写 SSE 解析而不上官方 SDK，有两个具体理由：
一是这一层必须能被评委完整读懂，过程可复现性是第一评分维度；
二是 record 模式要求「录下来的分片必须与当时推给前端的完全一致」，
只有自己攒分片才能保证，SDK 会把分片粒度藏起来。

usage 的字段名各家不统一（DeepSeek 的 prompt_cache_hit_tokens
与 OpenAI 的 prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens），两种都认。
usage 只用于展示与成本估算，不参与任何判定，所以这里宽容是安全的；
而配置缺失则相反——必须点名缺了哪几个环境变量，不能含糊。
```

c) feat(server): 按模式装配 LLM 客户端，默认离线回放
```
默认值定成 replay 而不是 live 是刻意的：复现指南要求「克隆下来就能跑」，
任何需要联网才能启动的默认值都会把评委挡在门外。
要真调模型必须显式设置 CROSSEXAM_MODE，这个动作本身就是一道确认。

record 模式的卡带目录与回放目录默认同一个，
这样录完立刻能在同一台机器上验证回放，少一个「录到哪去了」的坑。
```

d) test(server): 用假 fetch 覆盖真实调用与录制回放的闭环
```
真实调用的测试全程用假的 fetch，一个网络请求都不发——
否则测试会依赖网络与额度，评委复现时必然失败。

最后一条测试是闭环：录一遍、再放一遍，断言分片与思考过程都原样回来。
这是「离线可复现」这句话唯一算数的证明方式。
```

Let me define the schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  mode TEXT NOT NULL,              -- replay / record / live
  job_title TEXT NOT NULL,
  jd_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  resume_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  resume_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,            -- draft / probing / done / failed
  budget_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  meta_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS claim (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
  text TEXT NOT NULL,
  category TEXT NOT NULL,
  quote TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  verdict TEXT,
  advice TEXT,
  UNIQUE (session_id, ordinal)
);
CREATE INDEX ... claim_session ON claim(session_id, ordinal);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS probe (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES claim(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  interviewer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  round INTEGER NOT NULL,
  question TEXT NOT NULL,
  answer TEXT,
  answer_kind TEXT,        -- answered / unanswered / dodge
  verdict TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX probe_claim ON probe(session_id, claim_id, round);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  seq INTEGER NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL,
  payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, seq)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS llm_call (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  session_id TEXT,
  digest TEXT NOT NULL,
  purpose TEXT NOT NULL,
  model TEXT NOT NULL,
  replayed INTEGER NOT NULL,
  input_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
  output_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
  cache_read_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
  reasoning_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
  elapsed_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX llm_call_digest ON llm_call(digest);
```

Also `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON` is important, must be set per connection.

withTransaction: `db.exec('BEGIN')` ... commit/rollback. Note node:sqlite may not support nested; guard with a flag? Keep simple: no nesting, document it.

db.ts:
```ts
/**
 * SQLite 连接与迁移入口。
 *
 * 用 node:sqlite 而不是 better-sqlite3，是一条硬约束下的选择：
 * 赛题要求「10 分钟极速复现」，而带原生扩展的依赖在 Windows 上
 * 需要预编译包或本地编译工具链——评委的机器大概率没有。
 * Node 22.5 起内置 sqlite，代价是启动时一行 ExperimentalWarning。
 */

import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

export type Db = DatabaseSync;

export interface OpenOptions {
  /** 文件路径；':memory:' 表示内存库（测试用）。 */
  path: string;
  readonly?: boolean;
}

export function openDatabase(opts: OpenOptions): Db {
  const db = new DatabaseSync(opts.path, { readOnly: opts.readonly ?? false });
  // 外键必须每个连接单独打开，忘了就会出现"删了会话留下孤儿主张"。
  db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
  if (opts.path !== ':memory:') db.exec('PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL');
  db.exec('PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000');
  return db;
}

export function closeDatabase(db: Db): void { db.close(); }

export function withTransaction<T>(db: Db, fn: () => T): T {
  db.exec('BEGIN');
  try {
    const out = fn();
    db.exec('COMMIT');
    return out;
  } catch (err) {
    db.exec('ROLLBACK');
    throw err;
  }
}
```

Careful: `new DatabaseSync(path, { readOnly })` — option name is `readOnly`. Yes, node:sqlite uses `readOnly`.

schema.ts:
```ts
export const SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
export const DDL: readonly string[] = [...];
export function migrate(db: Db): number { ... }
```

`PRAGMA user_version` read: `const row = db.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get() as { user_version?: number } | undefined;`

Set: `db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = ' + String(SCHEMA_VERSION))`.

migrate: if current === 0 → run DDL in a transaction, set version. if current === SCHEMA_VERSION → return. if current > SCHEMA_VERSION → throw Error('数据库版本 ... 高于代码版本 ...').

Let me write it.

Also note: `db.exec` with multiple statements in one string — node:sqlite `exec` supports multiple statements. I'll still keep an array of statements for clarity and to attribute failures.

```text
33|
```

```ts
function parseJson<T>(text: string, fallback: T): T {

function bit(value: boolean): number { return value ? 1 : 0; }
function bool(value: unknown): boolean { return value === 1 || value === true; }
function str(value: unknown): string { return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''; }
function int(value: unknown): number { return typeof value === 'number' ? value : Number(value ?? 0); }
```

```js
const prog = String.raw`
## Session 3 — 2026-10-26（P1 下半：LLM 适配层与存储层）

### 做了什么

**LLM 适配层（packages/server/src/llm/）**

- live.ts：OpenAI 兼容 /chat/completions 客户端，SSE 自己按行解析。
  超时用 AbortController 且定时器挂在响应上（流读完才清，避免误杀正在读的流）；
  400/422 归为 bad-request，其余非 2xx 归为 http-error 且信息里带响应体前 400 字符。
  缺配置时抛 not-configured，**点名缺哪几个环境变量**，并提示可以切 replay——
```

```text
/**
 * 意图契约（创新点 3：Intent Contract）。
 *
 * 常规做法是把面试官的人设写成一大段散文 system prompt——它能跑，但没法评审、没法比对、
 * 没法回答「上一版和这一版到底改了什么」。出了坏结果只能靠肉眼看字符串。
 *
 * 这里把每个 Agent 的意图拆成**结构化字段**（身份 / 目标 / 允许的手法 / 硬约束 / 禁止行为 /
 * 预算），system prompt 由 renderSystemPrompt 从这些字段确定性渲染出来。
 * 于是：契约可以 diff、可以版本化、可以单测；每条追问都能追溯到「是哪一版契约生成的」。
 */

import type { InterviewerRole, Persona } from './domain.ts';
import { shortHash, stableStringify } from './util.ts';

export interface ContractBudgetView {
  maxRoundsPerClaim: number;
  maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim: number;
  maxTotalProbes: number;
}

export interface IntentContract {
  /** 面试官 id，与 Probe.interviewerId 对应 */
  id: string;
  /** 契约版本；任何字段改动都必须 +1，历史版本留在 ADR 与回放记录里 */
  version: number;
  persona: Persona;
  /** 这位面试官这一场想达成什么 */
  goal: string;
  /** 允许的追问手法 */
  tactics: readonly string[];
  /** 由代码保证、不可协商的硬约束 */
  constraints: readonly string[];
  /** 明确禁止的行为 */
  forbidden: readonly string[];
  budget: ContractBudgetView;
}

/** 所有面试官共享的硬约束——注意它们和 anchor.ts 的实现是一一对应的，不是嘴上说说 */
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a full picture. Let me design P2 orchestrator.

New directory: `D:\CZB\packages\server\src\orchestrator\`

Files:
1. `prompts.ts` — 提示词渲染（确定性）：buildExtractPrompt(resume, jd), buildProbePrompt(contract, claim, resume, priorProbes), buildAnswerPrompt(persona of candidate?), buildVerdictPrompt(claim, probes, answers), buildSuggestPrompt(claim, probes, verdict).
   Prompt text must be deterministic (no timestamps, no randomness) — otherwise cassette digest changes each run.
2. `extract.ts` — extractClaims({client, resume, jd, idFactory}) → {claims, rejected}
   - 调 LLM，parseLlmOutput(claimListSchema)，逐个 resolveAnchor；失败进 rejected
   - dedupe by anchor overlap? Keep simple: dedupe identical quotes.
   - limit claims count.
3. `panel.ts` — 组庭: buildPanel(size, roles) → IntentContract[]  (uses buildContracts + role selection: tech, hr, hiring_manager, stress, domain_expert)
4. `probe.ts` — askProbes({client, contracts, claims, budget, ...}) → 一轮并发追问
5. `answer.ts` — simulateAnswer (candidate agent answers only from resume; answered=false if not found)
6. `judge.ts` — judgeClaims → Verdict[]
7. `advise.ts` — suggest() → Suggestion[]
8. `run.ts` — runSession orchestrator: 串起全流程，发事件回调 onEvent(event)。

That's a lot. Given the session's remaining budget and that each file is large, I should split across multiple run_code calls and commits.

First: prompts.ts + extract.ts (one commit "feat(server): 抽取主张"). Actually prompts deserve their own commit: "feat(server): 用确定性模板渲染各阶段提示词".

Let me be pragmatic and design a smaller, coherent set of files:

- `orchestrator/prompts.ts`: all prompt builders (pure, deterministic).
- `orchestrator/pipeline.ts`? No — separate stages are better for commits.

Plan for P2 (server side):
1. `prompts.ts` (pure rendering, tested) — commit 1
2. `extract.ts` (主张抽取 + 锚定拒绝) — commit 2
3. `answer.ts` + `probe.ts`? Actually probe needs the contract + budget; answer is the "求职者 Agent".
   - `probe.ts` (一轮追问：并发调用多个面试官，过预算闸门) — commit 3
   - `answer.ts` (模拟作答：只依据简历) — commit 4
   - `judge.ts` (判定 + 反推建议，一起?) — commit 5, 6
   - `run.ts` (编排全流程 + 事件) — commit 7
4. http layer later.

To keep momentum, let me consider merging answer into probe file? No: answer is a distinct product concept (AI 扮演求职者，只依据简历作答 — this is what makes offline demo possible). Separate is better for the "≥3 core iterations" prompt chain evidence.

Let me define the LLM request shape carefully. LlmClient.complete(req) where req = {purpose, system, messages, temperature?, maxTokens?, json?}.

Each stage builds a request. The `purpose` label is for humans and excluded from digest.

Deterministic prompts: no Date.now, no ids derived from random.

Let me write prompts.ts now.

Design of prompts.ts:

```ts
import type { IntentContract, Claim, Probe, Answer, ResumeOutline } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { renderSystemPrompt, outlineForPrompt, VERDICT_LABEL } from '@crossexam/shared';
```

Export:
- `EXTRACT_SYSTEM: string` — 抽取官 system prompt（固定常量）
- `buildExtractPrompt(input: { resumeText; jdText; outline; maxClaims }): string`
- `PROBE_SYSTEM` maybe derived from contract via renderSystemPrompt + a fixed header.
- `buildProbePrompt(input: { claim; resumeText; outline; alreadyAsked: Probe[]; round; remaining: {rounds, quota} }): string`
- `buildAnswerPrompt(input: { resumeText; claim; probe }): string`
- `buildVerdictPrompt(input: { claim; probes: {probe, answer}[]; resumeText }): string`
- `buildSuggestionPrompt(input: { claim; verdict; probes }): string`

Templates: The prompts should be Chinese, structured, and *show the JSON contract*.

Important: the extract prompt must include the resume with **numbered segments** (outlineForPrompt) so that quotes are easy to copy verbatim... but careful: if the model copies from a truncated segment (with …), the quote won't resolve. Better: give the full resume text plus numbered outline for orientation. Full text is needed for verbatim quotes. Use outline (200 chars/segment) for orientation AND full text for quoting. Hmm, duplication. Simpler: give only full resume text with segment numbers prefixed? That would pollute the text since the numbers aren't part of the resume.

Decision: give the outline with a larger maxCharsPerSegment (600) for orientation, then the full resume under a "以下是简历全文，引用必须从这一段逐字复制" heading. Duplication is acceptable (~1-2k chars).

Actually simpler and defensible: only include full resume, with each segment prefixed by a marker line like `--- #3 [条目] ---`. Then quotes must be copied from the text below the marker (verbatim copy of resume text lines). The markers are clearly not part of the text. This avoids duplication and keeps the model oriented. But risk: model may include the marker in the quote → resolution fails → rejected. Mitigation: say explicitly 「不要把分隔行写进 quote」.

I'll do: full resume divided by markers, and instruct that quote must be a verbatim substring of the resume body, not including the marker lines. This is deterministic and compact.

Let me write `renderResumeForPrompt(outline)`:
```
#1 [小节] 教育经历
#2 [条目] 2021.09–2025.06 某某大学 计算机科学与技术 本科
...
```
Hmm that's outlineForPrompt but truncating at maxChars may cut mid-segment; quotes copied from truncated parts still resolve (since prefix is verbatim). Actually truncation only cuts the tail, so any copied prefix resolves fine. Wait — with the `…` appended, if the model copies up to the ellipsis, the quote "…" wouldn't be in the source. But it wouldn't copy the ellipsis.

So `outlineForPrompt(outline, 600)` is fine and simple, and cheap in tokens. But it truncates long segments, potentially hiding content worth probing. Compromise: `outlineForPrompt(outline, 400)`.

Hmm, but resumes are typically < 4KB. Let me just pass the FULL text with markers. Implementation: build from outline.segments: for each segment, `#N [条目]\n` + full segment text. That's complete and unambiguous. Use full text. Since the resume may have sections/links, fine.

Let me write that as a local function `renderResume(outline)` in prompts.ts (not in shared, to keep shared stable)... Actually it'd be natural in shared/segment.ts. But adding to shared requires touching shared and its tests. It's fine to put in prompts.ts.

Now JSON output shape in extract prompt:
{"claims":[{"text":"...","quote":"...","category":"metric","weight":0.9}]}

Probe prompt output: {"probes":[{"claimId":"...","question":"...","quote":"...","rationale":"..."}]}

Answer: {"answered":true,"text":"...","citedQuotes":["..."]}

Verdict: {"verdicts":[{"claimId":"...","kind":"solid","confidence":0.8,"rationale":"...","evidenceQuotes":["..."]}]}

Suggestion: {"suggestions":[{"claimId":"...","problem":"...","before":"...","after":"...","why":"..."}]}

Since we call one claim at a time for probe/verdict maybe, but batch is cheaper. With replay fixtures, each call needs a cassette. Batch per stage per claim is more granular (better for streaming demo: each interviewer question streams in separately). For probing, we want concurrency across interviewers → per-interviewer calls are natural: each interviewer asks about a set of claims (1 call per interviewer per round). That gives concurrent streams, which is the demo money shot.

Design for probing round r:
- for each contract (interviewer), call client.complete({purpose: 'probe:'+interviewerId, system: renderSystemPrompt(contract), messages:[user prompt with: claims list (open ones, with remaining budget), resume, already asked by this interviewer]}) → probeListSchema → filter by budget gate.
- Then orchestrate: for each accepted probe, ask the candidate agent (answer.ts) → answer.
- Then recordAnswer in budget → possibly close claim.
- Loop rounds until no open claims or budget exhausted.

This is a good, explainable design. Cost: 4 interviewers × R rounds calls. With R=3 → 12 calls + extract 1 + judge 1 + suggest 1.

That's a lot of cassettes to record without a key... but replay fixtures are meant to be recorded once with a real key. For the demo, I can write a **mock client** that produces canned JSON? No — better: the user may have an API key later. But for tests and offline demo we need fixtures. Hmm.

Important product decision (already made in ADR/budget): default replay requires fixtures. We don't have an API key → no real cassettes. Options:
(a) Ship hand-written fixtures for a demo resume (a "golden session" cassette set) so `npm run dev` works offline out of the box. This is legitimate: fixtures are checked in as the demo dataset.
(b) Provide a `CROSSEXAM_MODE=mock` deterministic fake LLM for tests only.

```ts
/**
 * 提示词渲染（纯函数、确定性）。
 * ...
 */
import type { Answer, Claim, IntentContract, Probe, ResumeOutline, Verdict } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { renderSystemPrompt, VERDICT_LABEL } from '@crossexam/shared';

const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);

export function renderResume(outline: ResumeOutline): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  for (const s of outline.segments) {
    const tag = s.kind === 'header' ? '[小节]' : s.kind === 'bullet' ? '[条目]' : '[段落]';
    lines.push('#' + s.index + ' ' + tag);
    lines.push(s.text);
    lines.push('');
  }
  return lines.join(NL).trim();
}
```

Now write the test helper (scripted client) and tests for prompts + extract. The scripted client is broadly useful: a `createScriptedClient(handlers)` where handlers is a function (req) => string, mode 'live'.

Put it in `packages/server/test/scripted.ts`:

```ts
import type { LlmClient, LlmRequest, LlmResult, LlmStreamEvent } from '../src/llm/types.ts';

export interface ScriptedCall { request: LlmRequest; }
export type Handler = (req: LlmRequest, index: number) => string | Error;

export function createScriptedClient(handler: Handler): LlmClient & { calls: LlmRequest[] } {
  const calls: LlmRequest[] = [];
  const client: LlmClient = {
    mode: 'live',
    async complete(req) {
      calls.push(req);
      const out = handler(req, calls.length - 1);
      if (out instanceof Error) throw out;
      return { text: out, usage: {...}, digest: 'scripted' + calls.length, replayed: false, elapsedMs: 0 };
    },
    async *stream(req) { ... }
  };
  return Object.assign(client, { calls });
}
```

Also a JSON convenience: `jsonHandler(map)` where map: purpose → string.

Concurrency: for each contract, one LLM call (parallel). Each call: choose the claim? One call per (interviewer, claim) pair would be N×M. Better: one call per interviewer covering **all open claims**, model returns probes for whichever claims it wants. Then budget checks per probe. Better: one call per interviewer per round with all open claims listed. Let me adapt: buildProbePrompt accepts `claims: Claim[]` instead of single claim. I need to update prompts.ts (which I just committed — a change is a new commit, fine: "refactor(server): 追问提示词支持一次覆盖多条主张").

Design: 
- Each interviewer call gets: list of open claims with per-claim notes (你已问过 x 次，全场还剩 y 轮，你的剩余额度 z), the interviewer's already-asked probes.
- Model returns up to `maxProbes` (say 2) probes referencing claimIds.
- Orchestrator validates each: claimId exists & open; anchor resolves on resume; budget canProbe allows; no duplicate question text; then consumeProbe.

For concurrency I'll use Promise.all over contracts, each catching errors → outcome. But if one interviewer's call fails (e.g., network), should the whole round fail? For robustness in live mode: catch per interviewer, record error, continue with others. But in replay mode a missing cassette should be loud... Hmm: missing cassette = misconfiguration; silently continuing would produce a session with no questions — bad. Decision: cassette errors (LlmError with code starting 'cassette-') propagate; other errors are collected per interviewer as failures, so one flaky interviewer doesn't kill the round. That's a defensible, explainable rule. 

Let me write the type: `CallOutcome`.

Also deterministic ordering: probes from Promise.all keep contract order; within a call, model order; filter preserves that. Good for replay determinism.

```
export function buildProbePrompt(input: {
  claim: Claim;
  resumeText: string;
  outline: ResumeOutline;
  asked: readonly Probe[];
  round: number;
  remainingRounds: number;
  quotaLeft: number;
}): string {

  lines.push('## 你要问的主张');
  lines.push(renderClaim(input.claim, '你还可以问 ' + input.quotaLeft + ' 次；这条主张全场还剩 ' + input.remainingRounds + ' 轮。'));
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 你此前问过的问题（不要重复，也不要换句话问同一个点）');

  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 简历原文（quote 必须从这里逐字复制，不要带 # 开头的编号行）');
  lines.push(renderResume(input.outline));
  return lines.join(NL);
}
```

```ts
export function buildProbePrompt(input: {
  claims: readonly Claim[];
  notes: Record<string, string>;
  resumeText: string;
  outline: ResumeOutline;
  asked: readonly Probe[];
  round: number;
  maxProbes: number;
}): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push('## 本轮是第 ' + input.round + ' 轮追问');
  lines.push('## 你可以追问的主张（只从这些里选，不要新造主张）');
  lines.push(renderClaims(input.claims, input.notes));
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 你此前问过的问题（不要重复，也不要换句话问同一个点）');
  lines.push(renderProbes(input.asked));
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 简历原文（quote 必须从这里逐字复制，不要带 # 开头的编号行）');
  lines.push(renderResume(input.outline));
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 本轮最多问 ' + String(input.maxProbes) + ' 条');
  lines.push('挑你最想问的。问不出有价值的问题就返回空数组，不要凑数。');
  return lines.join(NL);
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 阶段二：一轮追问。
 * ...
 */
import type { Claim, IntentContract, Probe, ResumeOutline } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { REJECTION_LABEL, canProbe, consumeProbe, parseLlmOutput, probeListSchema, resolveAnchor } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { BudgetState } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { LlmClient, LlmUsage } from '../llm/types.ts';
import { EMPTY_USAGE } from '../llm/types.ts';
import { LlmError } from '../llm/types.ts';
import { buildProbePrompt, probeSystem, stageRequest } from './prompts.ts';

export const MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN = 2;

export interface RejectedProbe { claimId: string; question: string; reason: string; }

export interface InterviewerOutcome {
  interviewerId: string;
  asked: number;
  rejected: RejectedProbe[];
  usage: LlmUsage;
  error: string | null;
}

export interface ProbeRoundResult {
  probes: Probe[];
  rejected: RejectedProbe[];
  outcomes: InterviewerOutcome[];
  budget: BudgetState;   // 记账后的账本
}
```

Implementation:

```ts
export interface ProbeRoundInput {
  client: LlmClient;
  contracts: readonly IntentContract[];
  claims: readonly Claim[];
  resumeText: string;
  outline: ResumeOutline;
  budget: BudgetState;
  round: number;
  history: readonly Probe[];  // 全场已问过的（用于提示词与去重）
  maxProbesPerTurn?: number;
}

export async function runProbeRound(input): Promise<ProbeRoundResult> {
  // 只把仍然开放的主张摆上桌
  const open = input.claims.filter(c => {
     const gate = canProbe(input.budget, c.id, input.contracts[0]?.id ?? '');
     ...
  });
```
Hmm, "open" determination: use `budget.claims[c.id].closed` or `openClaims(state)`. Use openClaims(budget) to get ids, then map to claims.

Then build notes per claim per interviewer: for each contract, notes[c.id] = '你的剩余额度 ' + quota. quota computation: canProbe per (claim, interviewer) returns remainingRounds (global) — and per-interviewer left = config.maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim - perInterviewer[id]. But note text is per interviewer, so notes must be built inside the per-contract loop. Good.

For each contract (parallel with Promise.all over map):
```ts
async function askOne(contract): Promise<{contract, drafts: ProbeDraft[], usage, error|null}> 
```
Then process results sequentially in contract order to keep budget deterministic.

Processing a draft:
1. find claim by id; if missing → rejected('模型引用了不存在的主张')
2. gate = canProbe(budget, claim.id, contract.id); if !allowed → rejected(gate.reason)
3. resolveAnchor(resumeText, {quote, start, end}); if !ok → rejected(REJECTION_LABEL + quote)
4. duplicate check: history + newly accepted probes with same (claimId, interviewerId, normalized question)? Or same question text across all → I'll dedupe on normalized question text regardless of interviewer: if seen → rejected('与已有提问重复').
5. accept: create Probe { id: 'P<n>' deterministic sequential, claimId, interviewerId: contract.id, round, question, anchor, parentProbeId: parents[claimId+interviewer] ?? null, rationale }
   - parentProbeId: link to the interviewer's previous probe on the same claim (makes a chain visible in UI). Use last accepted probe id for that (claimId, interviewerId).
   - consume budget.
   - maxProbesPerTurn cap per interviewer.

Probe id: 'P' + (history.length + accepted.length + 1)? Must be deterministic and unique. Use index from an incrementing counter starting at history.length + 1. Good.

Also cap total per round: maxProbesPerTurn per interviewer.

Also: what if the model returns claimIds for claims not open → rejected reason '该主张已关停，不再追问'? Just '不在本轮可追问列表中'.

Return outcomes with error per interviewer.

Cassette errors propagate: in askOne, catch error; if error instanceof LlmError and code startsWith('cassette') → rethrow. Also 'not-configured' should propagate. Let me define `isFatalLlmError(err)`: LlmError with code in ['cassette-missing','cassette-corrupt','cassette-version','not-configured','bad-request'] → fatal; 'http-error' → non-fatal (收集)? Hmm, http-error may mean the whole key is wrong; but per-interviewer collection is fine, and run.ts can surface. I'll treat 'http-error' as collectible.

Write it.

- 无锚点引文 → rejected，且不消耗预算。
- 预算拒绝：把 config 调成 maxTotalProbes=0 → 全部 rejected，reason 提到总次数。
- 重复问题去重（跨面试官）。
- 某个面试官调用失败（脚本化返回 Error）→ 其他面试官照常，outcomes 里 error 非空。
- 卡带类错误（LlmError cassette-missing）→ 整轮抛出，不静默变空。
- 第二轮 parentProbeId 指向第一轮同面试官同主张的 probe。
- 已关停的主张不再摆上桌。

```ts
export interface AnswerProbeInput {
  client: LlmClient;
  claim: Claim;
  probe: Probe;
  resumeText: string;
  outline: ResumeOutline;
  history: readonly Probe[];      // 其他面试官问过的（保持口径一致）
  mode?: AnswerMode;              // 'simulated'
}
export interface AnswerResult {
  answer: Answer;      // mode 'simulated'
  answered: boolean;
  usage, digest, raw
}
export async function answerProbe(input): Promise<AnswerResult>
```

Logic: call LLM with ANSWER_SYSTEM + buildAnswerPrompt. Parse answerDraftSchema. Then:
- If draft.answered === false → answer.text = draft.text (说明简历没写), citedAnchors = [].
- Else: resolve each citedQuote; keep those that resolve (drop unresolved silently but count as `droppedCitations` for honesty). Important: if answered=true but no citation resolves → that's the "编造" case. Do we force answered=false? Design decision: **答题时的锚定是软约束**：候选人的回答可以口述细节（面试里本来就允许口头解释），但引用简历原文时必须能定位。So: answered stays true, but keep a flag `grounded: boolean`? Hmm, adding fields to Answer type would require domain change.

Simplify: Answer.citedAnchors holds only successfully resolved anchors; unresolved ones are dropped and reported in the result (`droppedCitations: string[]`). Verdict stage then sees the answer text so it can judge. Honest and simple.

Also batch answering: one call per probe → a round with 4 probes = 4 calls. Acceptable (each answer is short). Concurrency: answers should be sequential? They're independent; but "保持口径一致" uses history of other questions — using concurrent answers means the模型 doesn't see other answers this round. Fine: history = probes asked before this round.

Decision: `answerProbes(input)` handling a batch, concurrently, collecting per-probe results. Because 4 probes per round × 3 rounds = 12 calls. Fine.

I'll provide both: `answerProbe` (single) and `answerRound` (concurrent batch). Keep one: `answerProbes({client, probes, claims, ...})` returns Map/array. Simplest: single `answerProbe`, and run.ts uses Promise.all. But then error handling duplicated in run.ts. I'll do the batch function with the same fatal/non-fatal policy (returns errors array).

Hmm, keep it lean: implement `answerProbe` (single, throws on fatal, returns error string otherwise?) — for a candidate answer, failure isn't fatal; return an "答不上来（系统故障）" answer? That would distort the verdict. Better: batch function collects failures separately, and failed probes get NO answer (stays unanswered), recorded in errors.

The flag matters only for budget accounting at run time.

So: NO domain change. `answerProbes` returns outcomes with `answered` for run-time use; the Answer saved is the text itself. That's a defensible minimal model: "答不上来" 是当时的一次记账，不是答案的属性。 Actually hmm, it IS a property of the answer...

I'll go with returning `{ answer, answered }` and persist only Answer — and note the rationale in a comment: the flag drives budget accounting at run time; the transcript stores what was actually said. Good enough, avoids a migration. And the `event` log will carry `answered` so it's recoverable.

```js
const content = String.raw`/**
 * 阶段三：求职者 Agent 作答。
 *
 * 这个 Agent 存在的理由不是"代替用户"，而是**让离线演示成为可能**：
 * 没有 API Key 的评委也能看到一场完整的多 Agent 对抗。
 * 它的纪律写在提示词里，也写在代码里：
 *
 * - 只依据简历作答。简历没写就说"简历里没有写"，**答不上来是被鼓励的诚实行为**。
 *   如果它每次都答得漂亮，这场压力测试就没有意义了。
 * - 引用简历原文时，引文同样要过锚定。定不到的引文被丢弃并单独记录——
 *   我们不改写它的回答（面试里本来就允许口头解释细节），
 *   但**不允许它伪造一句"简历原文"**。
 *
```

Now fix the three type errors.

1. line 95: `parsed.value.citedQuotes` possibly undefined → use `for (const quote of parsed.value.citedQuotes ?? [])`.
2. line 142: `item.error` — the union type. Use `'error' in item` narrowing should work... but `settled` items: `{probeId, error}` vs `{probeId, done}`. `'error' in item` narrows. Hmm TS should narrow. But because `as const` produces readonly union; `'error' in item` narrows to the first. Actually the error is at arg 142 col 44 = `message: item.error`. Maybe because Promise.all with async callbacks that throw makes the type include... The map callback returns `{probeId, error: '...'} | {probeId, done} | never`. TS narrowing with `in` on union works. Unless the union includes `{probeId: string; error: string} | {probeId: string; done: ...}` — should be fine.

Wait: maybe TS infers `item` as union of `{probeId:string; error:string}` and `{probeId:string; done:{outcome,usage}}`, and `'error' in item` narrows, but `item.error` might be `string | undefined` if one member has optional error... The callback for claim missing returns `{probeId, error: '...'}`; catch returns `{probeId, error: ...}`. Hmm both have error. Unless the `done` branch object also gets `error?: undefined`? No.

Possibly the issue: `Promise.all(input.probes.map(async (probe) => {...}))` where the async function returns a union that TS widens to `{probeId: string; error: string; done?: undefined} | {probeId: string; done: ...; error?: undefined}`? That widening does happen for object literal union normalization in some cases. Then `'error' in item` doesn't exclude the optional-undefined member, hence `string | undefined`.

Fix robustly: restructure to avoid union — use a result type with explicit field:

```ts
type Settled = { probeId: string; done?: { outcome: AnswerOutcome; usage: LlmUsage }; error?: string };
```
then `if (item.error) { errors.push({probeId: item.probeId, message: item.error}); continue; } if (!item.done) continue;`

Now write the test file. Use String.raw. Careful: content must avoid backticks and `${` inside String.raw. Test file will use template literals? I'll avoid them — use array join and JSON.stringify.

Handler routing: by question text in the prompt. buildAnswerPrompt includes the probe question. I'll route:

```ts
function handlerByQuestion(map: Record<string, string|Error>) {
  return (req) => {
    for (const key of Object.keys(map)) {
      if (req.messages.some(m => m.content.includes(key))) { const v = map[key]; return v; }
    }
    return new Error('没有为该问题准备返回值');
  };
}
```

Note LlmRequest has `messages` and `system`. Yes: LlmRequest{purpose,system,messages,...}.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now the remaining P2 core: verdict stage (`verdict.ts`) and suggestion stage (`suggest.ts`), then `run.ts` — the orchestrator entry that runs the whole session (extract → rounds of probe+answer → verdicts → suggestions) producing a session record with events, plus writing to the store.

Order: verdict.ts is the money stage (判定 + 证据链). Let's write it.

Verdict design:
- Input: client, claim, probes (all probes for that claim with their answers), resumeText, outline.
- Draft: verdictDraftSchema {kind, confidence, rationale, evidenceQuotes[]}.
- After parse: resolve each evidenceQuote → anchors (drop unresolved, record droppedEvidence).
- Also enforce consistency rules in code (not prompt):
  - If kind === 'solid' but there are zero resolved evidence anchors → downgrade? Careful: 'solid' means the claim holds; but with no evidence it's not solid. Design: 判定为 solid 需要至少一条能定位的证据；否则降级为 unsupported，并记录 reason（"模型给了站得住但拿不出可定位的证据，按证据缺失处理"）。This is a great "代码而非提示词" story — deterministic guard.
  - If kind === 'contradicts' but no resolved evidence → downgrade to unsupported too (与原文矛盾 must quote the conflicting原文).
  - 'unanswerable' requires no evidence — fine.
  - Also: if all answers were answered=false → force 'unanswerable'? That is a nice deterministic rule: 候选人一次都没答上来（且问答次数>0）→ unanswerable，无论模型怎么说。Hmm, but a candidate might refuse and that itself is "无法回答". Yes: rule — 所有追问都没答上（answer 非空 text?) Let's define: probes.length > 0 且 没有任何一次 answered=true → 强制 unanswerable。But the answer stage's `answered` flag doesn't persist in Answer... In run.ts we have outcomes with answered. So verdict input should carry `answeredProbeIds: string[]` or the run passes `answersWithFlag`. Simplify: VerdictInput gets `answers: readonly Answer[]` plus optional `unanswered: ReadonlySet<string>`... clunky.

Alternative: make verdict take `answered: readonly boolean[]` aligned with probes? Cleaner: pass an array of `{probe, answer, answered}` tuples. Let me define in verdict.ts:

```ts
export interface AnsweredProbe { probe: Probe; answer: Answer | null; answered: boolean }
export interface VerdictInput { client; claim; transcript: readonly AnsweredProbe[]; resumeText; outline }
```
Then force rule: transcript.length>0 && transcript.every(t => !t.answered) → kind='unanswerable', confidence high, rationale 由代码生成（"所有追问都没有得到有效回答"），并保留 droppedEvidence。That's exactly the kind of "意图控制" evidence the judges want.

Also confidence clamp: if downgraded, confidence = min(draft.confidence, 0.5)?

Write verdict.ts with:

```ts
export interface AnsweredProbe { probe: Probe; answer: Answer | null; answered: boolean }
export interface VerdictOutcome { verdict: Verdict; adjusted: boolean; adjustmentReason: string|null; droppedEvidence: string[] }
export interface VerdictClaimsInput { client; claims; transcriptByClaim: Record<string, readonly AnsweredProbe[]>; resumeText; outline }
export interface VerdictClaimsResult { outcomes: VerdictOutcome[]; errors: {claimId,message}[]; usage }
export const NO_EVIDENCE_REASON = '模型判定为「…」但拿不出可定位的原文证据，按证据缺失处理';
export function enforceVerdict(draft, resolvedEvidence, transcript, claimId): {verdict, adjusted, reason}
```

enforceVerdict rules order:
1. If transcript.length > 0 && every !answered → force unanswerable, adjusted=true, reason '候选人对这条主张的所有追问都没有给出有效回答，按「无法回答」处理' (even if model said solid). Evidence kept from resolved.
2. Else if (kind === 'solid' || kind === 'contradicts') && resolvedEvidence.length === 0 → downgrade to 'unsupported', reason.
3. Else keep.

Also clamp confidence to [0,1] (schema should already but clamp anyway with clamp()).

Also if kind === 'unsupported' and evidence empty → fine (证据缺失 is the default).

probeIds = transcript.map(t=>t.probe.id).

Write file.

suggest.ts design:
- Input: client, claim, verdict, probes, answers (transcript), resumeText, outline.
- Draft: suggestionDraftSchema {claimId, problem, before, after, why}.
- Code guards (important, and it's the "只改表述不加事实" enforcement):
  1. `before` must be locatable in resume → resolveAnchor. If not → the suggestion is dropped (recorded as rejected with reason). Because a rewrite whose "before" isn't in the resume can't be applied.
  2. Anchor for the suggestion = located anchor of before (or the claim's anchor as fallback).
  3. **不新增事实** check: 在 after 里出现的数字必须能在简历原文里找到（数字指纹比对）。This is a deterministic guard against fabricated numbers — very strong story. Implement: extract numeric tokens from after (regex /[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?/g plus Chinese numerals? keep to ASCII digits + common units), and require each appears in resumeText. If a new number appears → drop the suggestion? Or keep but flag? Dropping loses value; but "只改表述，不能加事实" — a suggestion that adds a number is invalid. So reject that suggestion and record reason "建议里出现了简历中没有的数字：X". That's honest and demonstrates control. Hmm, but a legit rewrite might add a number that's in the resume elsewhere (e.g. "日订单量 30 万" mentioned elsewhere) — we check against whole resumeText, so fine.
  4. Only produce suggestions for claims whose verdict kind is in a set {overstated, unsupported, contradicts}. For 'solid' → no suggestion needed (skip, code-side, no LLM call). For 'unanswerable' → suggestion is "补上口径/证据" — that's legitimate: 建议补写. So include unanswerable.
     Rule: kind === 'solid' → skipped (with reason '这条主张站得住，不需要改'). This saves calls and shows product thinking.
  5. after must differ from before (otherwise it's a no-op) → reject with reason '改写后与原文相同，等于没改'.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now write run.ts — the session orchestrator.

Design:

```ts
export interface RunSessionInput {
  client: LlmClient;
  resumeText: string;
  jdText?: string;
  panel: readonly InterviewerRole[];   // 默认 ['tech','hr','hiring_manager']
  budget?: BudgetConfig;
  maxRounds?: number;        // 默认 3（也用预算里的 maxRoundsPerClaim 约束）
  maxClaims?: number;
  maxProbesPerTurn?: number;
  onEvent?: (event: RunEvent) => void | Promise<void>;
  sessionId?: string;  // 事件里带上，便于前端关联
}
```

Flow:
1. outline = segmentResume(resumeText) — or accept outline.
2. contracts = buildContracts(panel, budgetView, ...)
3. emit 'stage' extract; claims = extractClaims
4. emit 'claims' {claims, rejected}
5. budgetState = initBudget(claims.map(id), config)
6. history: Probe[] = []
7. transcriptByClaim: Record<string, AnsweredProbe[]> = {}
8. loop round = 1..maxRounds:
   - emit 'probe-round-start'
   - probeResult = runProbeRound({..., budget: budgetState, round, history, maxProbesPerTurn})
   - budgetState = probeResult.budget
   - emit 'probes' {round, probes, rejected, outcomes}
   - if probes.length === 0 → break (理由：没有新追问 = 预算闸门或模型都无话可说)
   - emit 'answer-start'
   - answerResult = answerProbes({client, probes, claims, resumeText, outline, history})
   - emit 'answers' {outcomes}
   - for each probe in probes: answered = answeredFor(answerResult, probe.id); budgetState = recordAnswer(budgetState, probe.claimId, answered); transcriptByClaim[claimId].push({probe, answer: outcome?.answer ?? null, answered})
   - history.push(...probes)
   - if all claims closed → break
9. emit verdict stage: verdictResult = verdictClaims({client, claims, transcriptByClaim, resumeText, outline})
10. emit suggest stage: suggestResult = suggestClaims({client, claims, verdicts, transcriptByClaim, resumeText, outline})
11. return RunSessionResult {sessionId?, claims, rejected, contracts, budget: budgetSnapshot?, probes: history, transcriptByClaim, verdicts: Verdict[], verdictDetails, suggestions, notices, usage, rounds, events?}

Error handling: if extract throws → wrap? Let it throw. Fatal errors propagate. For the runner, a fatal (cassette-missing) must surface as an error but we should emit an event before throwing so the UI shows why. Let's catch fatal in run and emit 'error' then rethrow. Good.

Also record `notices` — e.g. answers errors, verdict errors, suggestions rejected — so UI can show "有 2 条没跑通".

Usage accumulation across stages.

Events: must be serializable and deterministic-ish. Types:
```ts
export type RunEvent =
 | {type:'stage'; stage: StageName; round?: number}
 | {type:'claims'; claims: Claim[]; rejected: RejectedClaim[]}
 | {type:'probes'; round:number; probes:Probe[]; rejected:RejectedProbe[]; interviewers: InterviewerOutcome[]}
 | {type:'answers'; answers: AnswerOutcome[]; errors: AnswerError[]}
 | {type:'budget'; totalProbes:number; closed:string[]}
 | {type:'verdicts'; verdicts: VerdictOutcome[]}
 | {type:'suggestions'; suggestions: SuggestOutcome[]}
 | {type:'error'; fatal:boolean; message:string}
 | {type:'done'; ...}
```
Note: since budget.recordAnswer drives closing, when a claim is closed mid-round we can emit 'budget' event.

Determinism for replay: the *decisions* of the code are deterministic; events contain no timestamps (timestamps added by the store layer). Good for fixture-based tests.

Test for run.ts with scripted client: a full happy path with 2 interviewers × 1 round, then check:
- verdicts produced for each claim
- suggestions produced
- budget closes claims after consecutive unanswerable
- events sequence order
- probe loop stops when maxRounds reached
- fatal error → emits error event and rethrows

Scripted handler needs to route by purpose AND content:
```ts
function router(req) {
  switch(req.purpose) {
    case 'extract': return claimsJson
    case 'probe': return byInterviewer...
    case 'answer': ...
    case 'verdict': ...
    case 'suggest': ...
  }
}
```
Purpose is set by stageRequest — good, use req.purpose.

Also guard: if claims.length === 0 → return early with empty results (no verdict calls, no suggestions), with a notice '没有抽到可被追问的主张'. Good edge case for tests and for the demo (LLM returned nothing).

- 说明：Write the full session orchestrator
- 代码：
```js
const content = String.raw`/**
 * 一次完整的质询沙盘。
 *
 * 这里是"多 Agent 对抗"真正的样子：抽取 → N 轮并发追问 → 作答 → 判定 → 反推建议。
 * 它被刻意写成一个纯函数式的编排层：不碰数据库、不碰 HTTP、不碰时间，
 * 只依赖注入进来的 LlmClient。于是同一份输入在 live / record / replay 三种模式下
 * 走的是**同一条代码路径**，差别只在客户端从哪儿拿文本。
 * 数据库与 SSE 由调用方接在 onEvent 上——回放时事件序列逐字节可比。
 *
 * 循环终止靠三重保险，缺一不可：
 *   1. round 上限（编排层的保险丝）
```

```ts
export function probeableClaims(state: BudgetState, interviewerIds: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];

    for (const id of interviewerIds) {
      if (canProbe(state, claimId, id).allowed) { out.push(claimId); break; }

  }

}
```

```ts
    expect(openClaims(s)).toEqual(['c1']);
    expect(probeableClaims(s, ['tech'])).toEqual([]);
    expect(probeableClaims(s, ['tech', 'hr'])).toEqual(['c1']);
  });
```
Note BudgetConfig requires all 4 fields. Use DEFAULT_BUDGET spread? Simpler: `{ ...DEFAULT_BUDGET, maxRoundsPerClaim: 1 }`. That's cleaner.

Also a total-budget case:
```ts
  it('全场总额度用尽后，任何主张都不再可追问', () => {
    let s = initBudget(['c1', 'c2'], { ...DEFAULT_BUDGET, maxTotalProbes: 1 });
    s = consumeProbe(s, 'c1', 'tech');
    expect(probeableClaims(s, ['tech', 'hr'])).toEqual([]);
  });
```
And recordAnswer-based closure: after 2 unanswerable, closed → not probeable. Maybe enough with 2 tests.

Need imports: probeableClaims, openClaims — check the import line. openClaims is likely already imported (used in an earlier test?). Let me grep imports and then edit.

My assumption about canProbe ordering was wrong: with maxRoundsPerClaim=1, the round check rejects for ALL interviewers (it's per-claim, not per-interviewer). So hr also blocked. Correct: rounds gate is claim-wide.

So fix test: use maxRoundsPerClaim default (3) and instead exhaust tech's per-interviewer quota (2 probes) → openClaims false? no, claim still open; probeable with ['tech'] → [] while with ['hr'] → ['c1'].

```ts

    let s = initBudget(['c1']);
    s = consumeProbe(s, 'c1', 'tech');
    s = consumeProbe(s, 'c1', 'tech'); // tech 的每人每主张额度是 2

    expect(probeableClaims(s, ['tech'])).toEqual([]);
    expect(probeableClaims(s, ['tech', 'hr'])).toEqual(['c1']);
  });
```

```ts
export async function runAndPersist(input: PersistRunInput): Promise<RunSessionResult> {
  const { db, sessionId } = input;
  const session = getSession(db, sessionId);
  if (session === null) throw new Error('会话不存在：' + sessionId);
  const emit = (e: RunEvent) => {
    appendEvent(db, sessionId, e.type, e as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
    input.onEvent?.(e);
  };
  updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'extracting');
  let result: RunSessionResult;
  try {
    result = await runSession({
      client: createRecordingClient(input.client, db, sessionId, () => { /* status hook */ }),
      resumeText: session.resumeText,
      jdText: session.jdText,
      panel: input.panel, budgetConfig: input.budgetConfig,
      maxRounds: input.maxRounds, maxClaims: input.maxClaims, maxProbesPerTurn: input.maxProbesPerTurn,
      onEvent: emit,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'failed');
    throw err;
  }
  persistResult(db, sessionId, result);
  updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'done');
  return result;
}
```
Status transitions on stage events: map stage → status: extract→extracting, probe→probing, verdict/suggest→scoring. Nice touch. Do it in emit: on stage event, updateSessionStatus accordingly.

```ts
import type { BudgetConfig, InterviewerRole } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { defaultCassetteDir } from './llm/cassette.ts';

const STAGE_STATUS: Record<string, SessionStatus> = {
  extract: 'extracting', probe: 'probing', answer: 'probing', verdict: 'scoring', suggest: 'scoring',
};

export interface RecordingOptions {
  db: Db;
  sessionId: string | null;

  cassetteDir?: string;

 * 这一层存在的意义：让"这次演示到底联没联网、花了多少 token"
 * 变成数据库里可查询的事实，而不是文档里的一句自称。
 */
export function withAccounting(inner: LlmClient, opts: RecordingOptions): LlmClient {
  function note(req: LlmRequest, result: LlmResult): void {
    recordLlmCall(opts.db, {
      sessionId: opts.sessionId,
      digest: result.digest,
      purpose: req.purpose,
      model: result.model? no...
```

withAccounting:
```ts
export function withAccounting(inner: LlmClient, opts: RecordingOptions): LlmClient {
  const mode = inner.mode;
  function note(req: LlmRequest, result: LlmResult): void {
    recordLlmCall(opts.db, {
      sessionId: opts.sessionId,
      digest: result.digest,
      purpose: req.purpose,
      model: opts.model ?? '',
      replayed: mode === 'replay',
      usage: result.usage,
      elapsedMs: result.elapsedMs,
    });
  }
  return {
    mode,
    async complete(req) { const r = await inner.complete(req); note(req, r); return r; },
    async *stream(req) {
      let last: LlmResult | null = null;
      for await (const ev of inner.stream(req)) {
        if (ev.type === 'done') { last = ev.result; }
        yield ev;
      }
      if (last) note(req, last);
    },
  };
}
```
Note: `replayed: mode === 'replay'` — actually result.replayed exists. Use result.replayed for accuracy. Yes LlmResult has replayed. Use that.

Careful with async generator in an object literal implementing LlmClient interface — stream returns AsyncIterable<LlmStreamEvent>. Fine. But TS: `async *stream(req: LlmRequest)` inside object literal typed as LlmClient — OK.

However the accounting wrapper is created per session run in runAndPersist with sessionId.

Then:
```ts
export interface PersistRunInput {
  db: Db;
  sessionId: string;
  client: LlmClient;
  model?: string;
  panel?: readonly InterviewerRole[];
  budgetConfig?: BudgetConfig;
  maxRounds?: number;
  maxClaims?: number;
  maxProbesPerTurn?: number;
  onEvent?: (event: RunEvent) => void | Promise<void>;
}

export async function runAndPersist(input: PersistRunInput): Promise<RunSessionResult> {
  const session = getSession(input.db, input.sessionId);
  if (session === null) throw new Error('会话不存在：' + input.sessionId);
  const db = input.db;
  const sessionId = input.sessionId;

  async function emit(event: RunEvent): Promise<void> {
    if (event.type === 'stage') {
      const next = STAGE_STATUS[event.stage];
      if (next) updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, next);
    }
    appendEvent(db, sessionId, event.type, event as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
    if (input.onEvent) await input.onEvent(event);
  }

  updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'extracting');
  try {
    const result = await runSession({
      client: withAccounting(input.client, { db, sessionId, model: input.model }),
      resumeText: session.resumeText,
      jdText: session.jdText,
      panel: input.panel,
      budgetConfig: input.budgetConfig,
      maxRounds: input.maxRounds,
      maxClaims: input.maxClaims,
      maxProbesPerTurn: input.maxProbesPerTurn,
      onEvent: emit,
    });
    persistResult(db, sessionId, result);
    updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'done');
    return result;
  } catch (err) {
    updateSessionStatus(db, sessionId, 'failed');
    throw err;
  }
}
```

```ts
    // 重新跑同一会话时先清掉旧结果：主张/追问/事件都有外键，删了会级联
    ...
  });
}
```
